| The engine refuses to open or align a bot's main session | Tries again with backoff, an opening under a fresh key, and shows a `session-not-ready` notice once three attempts in a row failed, until an alignment succeeds ([Sessions](#sessions), Every bot has a main session) |
| The engine refuses to archive a retired session | Tries again with backoff under fresh keys; the session holds no turn slot, and the rotator goes on with the others ([Sessions](#sessions), Rotation) |
| The engine refuses a bot's permission mode, to open or align its main session or to run a turn there | Neither opens, aligns nor plans into its main session until the bot's settings change or the engine becomes ready again, when it asks once more; shows a `permission-mode-refused` notice, naming the mode the policy allows when the engine reports it, once per refusal that differs from the last one told, and each of the bot's rooms a `member-mode-refused` notice; the rest of a room goes on without the bot. A message whose turn it refused waits with the new ones, in its place, and is delivered once after either; a refusal from an engine that has stopped since is not recorded ([Sessions](#sessions), Every bot has a main session) |
| The engine refuses a settings change over background work | Sends the bot no work, shows a `settings-held` notice once per change, offers the Stop, and tries again every 30 seconds; a Stop ends the work, and the change applies then ([Sessions](#sessions), Settings alignment) |
| An event cannot be applied | Logs it with its cause, shows an `observation-failed` notice once, sends nothing into the session meanwhile, and reads it again each time the stream reopens with backoff; one item's failure never stops the loop that met it |
| A worker fails unexpectedly | Logs it; the item that failed gets a later deadline and the pass goes on, and a worker whose pass failed waits thirty seconds before the next ([The workers](#the-workers)) |
| The push service cannot be reached | Sends the push again with backoff, within its bounds, then drops it and logs it; the roster still shows the entry unread ([Notifications](#notifications)) |
| A turn fails | Records the engine's error class on the dispatch and shows it in plain words, with what the operator can do. The engine's own words (the error's class, code and message) go with the notice behind Details, and to the server log, scrubbed of secrets by the adapter (tokens, keys, credentials in a URL) and cut to 2,000 characters. A routine run's attempt shows nothing and is tried again instead, up to three attempts ([Routines](#routines)). So is one whose side session the engine refuses to open, for the bot's model or otherwise |
| The engine tries a turn's AI again | Shows the bot retrying while it does; nothing is recorded or shown once a retry works ([4. The turn is observed](#4-the-turn-is-observed-and-admitted)) |
| An AI ignores a Stop | Offers Force stop, which has the engine end that turn's program ([Stop](#stop), Force stop) |
| A provider hits its limit | Marks the provider's bots blocked with the reset time; holds automated wakes until then. The turn that hit it stays open as a pending continuation, and a dispatch already waiting in the engine starts at the reset |
| The server process exits | On start, runs the sequence above, which also sends a dispatch written and never sent, whether or not its bot has a wake pending, and recovers every dispatch that was sent but has no recorded answer. No turn starts twice, because a dispatch keeps its key and its envelope |
| A session cannot be resumed | The engine runs the turn on its own summary of the old conversation; the server then rotates the session once it is quiet, and the new one starts from memory and the digest ([Sessions](#sessions)) |

### Known limits

What the pinned engine does not let J3 Bots see, and what it costs:

- **A delegated task's report can be answered twice when the engine hands it over just as the turn it reached completes.** The engine then sends the report again as a turn of its own, from the failed hand-over, after the turn has ended (**Read** at `bc0f04d`, `EffectWorker.ts`, L366-423). If the adapter sees that turn end before the new turn appears, it tells the report as having stayed there ([Rooms](#rooms)); the bot is woken for it in the delegating conversation and also answers it in the engine's own turn. Telling the two apart would need the engine to record that the hand-over failed, and it does not: the report's record in the turn stays as handed over on Codex, and moves on as if read on Claude Code (`ClaudeAdapterV2.ts`, L14069). It needs the provider to finish its turn in the moment the report is handed over. The same failure on an operator's steer to Claude Code counts the steer as taken (`steer-taken`) before the engine runs it as a turn of its own, so the turn it reached may message one more teammate than the hop limit allows.

- **A send whose answer was lost can hold a bot for up to a day once its settings change.** The recovery resends such a dispatch until the engine answers ([3. A dispatch is written](#3-a-dispatch-is-written-before-the-engine-is-called)), but not into a session whose settings wait for alignment: the engine would run it on the old ones. Alignment skips a session on a provider the bot left, and a change on the same provider can be held by background work there, whose end the Stop asks for. That Stop waits for the dispatch in flight to settle, and the rotation waits for the Stop. Only the recovery's one-day bound then ends it: the dispatch fails `unresolved`, the Stop settles, and the session rotates or aligns; until then the bot takes no work. It needs a lost answer and a settings change before the recovery resolves it. Settling such a dispatch without a resend would need the engine to say whether it holds a key without applying the command, and it cannot: no request of its socket reads a command's receipt alone, and a command it still holds before applying it is not in its projection (**Read** at `bc0f04d`: the socket's methods, `ORCHESTRATION_V2_WS_METHODS` in `contracts/orchestrationV2.ts`, offer none, and the receipts stay inside the server, in `CommandReceiptStore.ts`). Letting the Stop or the rotation go on past such a send would leave the session's old settings in reach of a key that may yet land.
